**The pre-cut fixes** (rev 8; each from its commit's body, `8b6ea6a7..38649f7e`)
- `48f2b9a6` (N-MCP-2): a refused redirect at bring-up is described by `ConfigInvalid`'s shared sentence ("could not be parsed … the file needs repair", action `Repair`), pinned by ext design §7.1's table row — its own wording is a design-row change and needs a plan entry; a reconnect inside a session re-handshakes with no outer deadline, so a server that accepts TCP and never answers holds it until the idle bound (300 s or more) or the sandbox's cut, not 30 s — N-MCP-1 owns it; the connect bound is checked on the builder only, not on the wire; a mid-session redirect refusal is returned to the model and leaves the row as it is. *Rev 20:* the reconnect's missing deadline closed by N-MCP-1 (`f010f0a8`): one deadline, `request_timeout`, spans the pre-send reconnect and the call.
- `f6c11897` (N-LEAD-1): a subagent task still not dropped when the abort's 1 s grace runs out closes its lane when the runtime drops it, possibly after the report was read; a plugin agent aborted mid-RPC is not sent `executor.stop` (Z4-12, beside M60).
- `7461ab0b` (N-LEAD-2): a queued subagent's lane reads `running` (§3.11); `estimated_llm_capacity`'s stale doc (`crates/openalpaca_llm/src/routing/router/capacity.rs:23-24`); the queue bound and the router's per-key concurrency are §1.13's. *Rev 9:* both taken 2026-10-19 (PDT; 2026-10-20T00:26Z) (§1.13): the queue bound as N-LEAD-6, the per-key concurrency as N-LLM-1, which also deletes `estimated_llm_capacity`; neither built.
- `b417eea1` (N-LEAD-4): an open lane is not closed in the log at resume (a later resume reads it as interrupted again; the boot sweep closes its span row); a lane's `detail` — a plugin agent's own failure output, a router error carrying a provider's body — stays uncapped in the session log's close record, and no longer reaches a system note: the resume note names each lane in fixed words. (The landing's report files this under N-TOOL-7; `b417eea1`'s body records it.)
- `6423a084` (N-LEAD-5): the `PrimaryFallback` case (§3.7); a plugin agent's failure text and the dispatcher's `Lead agent failed: {:?}` are as they were.
- `ab7a2e65` (N-TOOL-5): `crates/openalpaca_plugins/src/bridge/provider_bridge.rs:224-226` still turns unparsable tool arguments into `{}` — the unwired provider bridge, whose deletion is M86; the JSON-string call is §1.13's. *Rev 9:* that call is taken 2026-10-19 (PDT; 2026-10-20T00:26Z) as the sandbox's one decode before the sanitizer, N-TOOL-8 (§1.13); not built.
- `f6cf46c0` (N-TOOL-7): an `Err` over the threshold is shown its head and tail inline with no reference, and a replay hands it back as the head-only stub with one (`replay::result_text`'s doc says so); recording the stub's form for an `Err` too would close it.
- `8c63804f` (N-SKILL-2): unknown frontmatter keys are reported at the top level only; a plugin skill's slash claim is not compared; a broken hot edit leaves the skill listed while every invocation fails until the file is fixed — no cache of the last good instructions, the lead's ruling; the shipped skills' count is pinned at 4 (`the_shipped_skills_scan_clean`, `crates/openalpaca_core/src/orchestrator/skill/catalog/diagnostics_tests.rs:602-605`); for D4 (P30), a null top-level key fails the parse under serde-saphyr, and under serde_yaml a top-level unknown key carrying a custom tag fails once a flatten field exists. *Rev 22:* since P30 (`f0fc0436`) serde-saphyr reads the frontmatter: `~: x` is refused ("cannot deserialize null into string"), measured with the capture, and an unknown key whose value is `!custom v` now loads.
- `07f0c90c` (N-PLUG-4): a crash between `reset_consent` and the commit puts the old tree back pending — a decision a capability change dropped stays dropped, and the owner approves the old tree again (the swap marker records no decision, on purpose).
- `1a5512dc` (N-PLUG-6): a plugin skill still inserts over a file skill's key — B-05's commit (§1.12); so, by the landing's report, is the by-id removal through which a hot-reloaded file template replaces whatever holds its id (`apps/openalpacad/src/hot_reload.rs:606`).
- `eb6b41fc` (N-PLUG-2): the manifest preview's unescaped fields (§3.3), pre-existing; the plugin connector bridge's deletion needs a plan entry (§4.3).
- `08831341` (N-DAEMON-1): `main.rs`'s `set_shutdown_token` and the wake stage's `within` call are pinned by no test (no test boots `async_main`); the other three stages keep their inline "what is left" arithmetic.
- `38649f7e` (N-STORE-1): `state/` and `state/cache/` themselves are followed — only `open/` and below are no-follow; an earlier build's `$TMPDIR/openalpaca-open/` is not swept.
- `c7aebb98` (the MCP hang tests): the production supervisor still kills only a stdio server's direct child — P19's process group; a Ctrl-C'd `cargo test` runs no `Drop`, so a hanging stub lingers for at most its 30 s sleep; the daemon suite needs `perl` on PATH. *Rev 20:* P19 (`93463cc6`) built the group: a disabled stdio server takes the processes it started with it, a `setsid` escapee aside.

**Elsewhere**

(CLAUDE.md, daemon-lifecycle paragraph) *Rev 5:* the forced exit's `remove_discovery()` and each shutdown stage's bound are the fix N-DAEMON-1 (audit Z4-15; plan-rev3 §5.4). *Rev 8:* N-DAEMON-1 landed as `08831341`: the connector and wake stages each run within the remaining window, ending 1 s before the force-exit instant, and a forced exit removes `discovery.json` first; `c7aebb98` makes the MCP hang tests' stubs leave no orphaned sleep. In-flight work is still not drained, and the confirmation half is unchanged.

(deps plan §3.3) *Rev 5:* the ladder is the fix N-MCP-1 (plan-rev3 §5.4), and `client_info` part of §4.3's *P19 protocol key*. *Rev 7:* that key is taken (b), so P19 sends `client_info`. *Rev 18:* the README's OpenSSL headers fixed by P13 (`07aa9980`): its Linux line is `sudo apt-get install -y libssl-dev pkg-config`. *Rev 20:* the ladder ended by N-MCP-1 (`f010f0a8`), and `client_info` sent since P19 (`93463cc6`). *Rev 21:* since P28 (`b01001d3`) nothing links OpenSSL and the README's Linux line is `sudo apt-get install -y pkg-config`; the two rustls crypto backends are one installed provider since P41 (`c335727e`: aws-lc-rs at daemon start), ring still compiled on teloxide's reqwest 0.12 path until teloxide 0.18 or an upstream feature.

The reason: it is pre-existing, the daemon's port is dynamic, so nothing narrower can be pinned at build time, and plan-rev3 §7.8 allows no loosening, which this is not. Narrowing — a runtime-pinned `connect-src`, or daemon CORS restricted to the Tauri origins plus the dev origin — is revisited once Q04 lands. (review C-24; §4.3 D9)

## 6. Options a settled rule excludes — what ZCode does that OpenAlpaca deliberately does not (rev 5)

These are options for the owner, never defects (audit §6, which carries the ZCode citations). Each names the OpenAlpaca rule that excludes it today; adopting one is a reversal the owner makes explicitly. When a ZCode mechanism is borrowed, its trust default is not (audit §2 item 8). *Rev 7:* the owner's blanket answer of 2026-10-17 (PDT; 2026-10-18T01:56Z) took the recommendation on every row — keep the rule — so each row says so in its last column; nothing reverses a settled rule.

| ZCode does | Excluded by | Answer (rev 7) |
|---|---|---|
| Memory recall chosen by an auxiliary model reading a manifest; extraction as an agent loop (Z8-09) | MD-1: memory search is lexical, with no model on any memory path | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Skills loaded into the running loop, carrying no tool grants and no permission policy | The skill-isolation design (P1-1, Routing V2): a skill is a sandboxed sub-loop with declared tools, its own confirmation list, scripts and cron | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A per-skill enable toggle keyed by path (Z3-14) | The two-axis rule (ALLOW per agent, ENABLE per extension, no third axis); `invoke.mode: disabled` is the switch | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Per-tool deny lists and project allow/ask/deny rulesets matched by name and content (T1) | The two-axis rule and C8's purge of the per-tool deny key | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Session-wide permission modes (build, plan, yolo, guarded), yolo and guarded allowing before the deny list is read | Per-agent capabilities; no global bypass; the approval rules | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Auto-allowing a read-only shell command checked by AST against safe-flag tables (Z1-20) | ST-14: every shell command asks | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Tool paths not confined to the workspace | R31/R32's confinement — not to be relaxed toward ZCode | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A shell command backgrounded at its budget instead of killed (Z1-18) | R45: killed with its process group at the timeout (a later option with a design, §4.7) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| An unanswered question auto-continued with an empty answer after 5 minutes | A confirmation timeout is a denial (local-model round 10's T1) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A run-wide or project-wide persisted allow | R43: one tool, one subagent (an explicit reversal only, §3.3) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Install enables a plugin | ADR-030: installing grants nothing, and approve is the one act that starts a plugin | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Third-party plugin hooks run unconditionally, their children inheriting the agent's environment | OpenAlpaca has no hooks; a deny-capable hook would be a new enforcement point (Z2-14, §4.7) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Sensitive plugin config written in plain text | D1 and T9's `secret_encrypted` | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A git-tracked workspace config that can enable plugins and inject secrets | ST-1 and ST-9: nothing is read from a project folder | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Uninstall deletes the plugin's data | Ext design §12.1 (Phase 8's uninstall verb): an uninstall moves the tree to `plugins/.trash/` and keeps the data | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Project-scope MCP servers trusted and auto-connected | T8's recorded answer: such a declaration enters unapproved | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| UI plugins as MCP Apps (Z2-15) | MCP resources are not implemented, and serving MCP is a non-goal | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| No cost budget anywhere | N4: caps per turn ($1) and per workflow | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A subagent inherits bypass or auto modes and cannot lower them; a run with no recorded mode falls back to YOLO | The approval rules: an unattended turn refuses (M6), and there are no silent permissive defaults | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Subagents may never delegate | OpenAlpaca allows delegation to depth 3 (`crates/openalpaca_core/src/runner/lead_agent/tools.rs:71`; rev 9: `:208` at `2e02f684`, `MAX_SUBAGENT_DEPTH`) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A confirmation window on every workflow start | Routing V2: chat by default; chat against workflow is the model's own tool choice | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A fat Electron shell; agents die with the app; no CSP on the main renderer; a preload bridge of about 1,000 lines (Z6-15) | The thin shell (G1), a daemon that outlives the window (plan-rev3 §7.1, §7.8), a strict CSP (`apps/openalpaca-gui/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:24`) and M71–M75 | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| An overlay title bar | (c)-5 and DESIGN_SPEC §3.1: the decorated window | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17); (c)-5 taken as (a), §4.3 |
| A server process per session, its working directory the workspace (Z5-18) | The storage design's Stage 2: the daemon's directory is MCP servers' working directory | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| Close-to-hide on macOS (Z6-12) | Tauri's default and today's tested quit and relaunch; an option after Q10 (§4.7) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A "data storage path" setting that moves only part of the data | D1: one root | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A credential cipher whose fallback key derives from platform, home and user name | OpenAlpaca's random `state/.master_key` | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| mise, with pins that float across files (Z7-13) | Single-source pins that CI reads (`rust-toolchain.toml`, `packageManager`) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |
| A default pre-push hook running lint, the architecture check and affected tests (Z7-17) | The full suite takes about 5.5 minutes and agent sessions commit often; at most an opt-in format hook (§4.7) | Kept the rule — option not taken (2026-10-17) |

Where ZCode is behind, nothing is to be taken: it has no release-age rule and no advisory scanning (OpenAlpaca has `bunfig.toml`'s minimum release age and P34), and no hidden-character escaping on approval surfaces (OpenAlpaca's escape set) — so the hidden-character questions of §1.10 are not informed (audit §5.5).
